# API — Suzerain variable catalog

What each live/save variable key does. Keys are the in-game Lua variable
names (`BaseGame.*`, `RiziaDLC.*`, …); `Field` is the friendly name used
by `Suzerain-Save-Editor` and (planned) `BagOfTricks` presets.

> Source: `Suzerain-Save-Editor/Utils/Consts.py` (auto-generated — do not
> hand-edit tables; re-run `Utils/gen_api.py` after changing the source).
> Status: UNCONFIRMED against live game — save-editor maps are
> ~1yr old and mined saves are .153/.137-era while installed game is
> 3.1.0.1.175. Confirm each key live via `Agents/RESEARCH.md` before
> relying on it. Installed game: 3.1.0.1.175 (Steam build 23568265).
> Mechanism CONFIRMED against .175 dummy assemblies: `DialogueLua`.
> `GetVariable`/`SetVariable`, `LuaTable.Dict`, `LuaNumber.Number: double`.

Legend: `_field` (leading underscore) = internal/derived flag, not a
direct HUD stat. `—` in Key = composite UI field (see
`Models/Sordland.py` / `Models/Rizia.py` in save-editor), not a raw key.
Roles (from dialogue write/read sites + campaign variance): `store` = core
read-write state; `trigger` = one-shot event flag; `lever` = enactment flag;
`mirror` = engine-written, varies; `gate` = read-only branch input;
`record` = written, never branched on; `const` = static clamp/sentinel;
`dormant` = static, unreferenced (rare-branch or dead).
